# Skills · S · part 231

916 entries, spell-it-out to spezial-docx-tatbestand-beweis-und-belege. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **spell-it-out**: Explain one mechanism by tracing one concrete case through it, as one visual page read at a glance that ends in the decisions left open. Use when the user asks to explain how X works, why X failed or missed a case, or to show them the mechanism behind a behaviour, and wants to understand it or decide on it rather than have it fixed, or when open questions about one mechanism go to the owner as a decision page. One mechanism, one case; a whole layer's inventory belongs to architecture-report. ([mknw/muster-skills](https://github.com/mknw/muster-skills/tree/HEAD/spell-it-out) · ★2)
- **spell-it-out**: Spell-it-out shape for a technical stop, where the agent waits on the person (a yes/no, a choice, an approval gate, a "should I…?") and the answer turns on code, tools, data or deploys, so someone who does not read code can still answer it well. Use before any such question that ends a turn, inside any other skill's stop, and when the person says "spell it out", "I don't get it" or "what does that mean". ([laurenbdaniels-coder/townsend-plugins](https://github.com/laurenbdaniels-coder/townsend-plugins/tree/HEAD/spell-it-out/skills/spell-it-out) · ★1)

- **spelling-and-grammar**: Use whenever the user writes to you in English — before acting on their message, check it for grammar and spelling errors. If there are any, point them out visually (❌ error → ✅ fix), show the corrected version, then treat the corrected version as the actual request and proceed. If the message is clean, say nothing about it and just proceed. Applies to every English message, every time — not only when the user asks for a proofread. Triggered when the user wants to memorize a conversation, learn from a document, or capture specific requirements/decisions outside of the active feature orchestration flow. ([ran729/context-rot-skill](https://github.com/ran729/context-rot-skill/tree/HEAD/.claude/.claude/skills/spex-learn) · ★2)
- **spex-onboard**: Analyze codebase to identify applications and libraries, then use spex to persist them ([ran729/context-rot-skill](https://github.com/ran729/context-rot-skill/tree/HEAD/.claude/.claude/skills/spex-onboard) · ★2)
- **spex-plan**: Creates a comprehensive implementation plan md file for large-scale, complex features or significant system changes across one or more specs. Use for breaking down major initiatives into development tasks, scoping high-effort engineering work, and planning multi-phase architectural rollouts. ([foreachpartners/main-spex](https://github.com/foreachpartners/main-spex/tree/HEAD/examples/shopping-notes/shopping-notes-spex/.agents/skills/spex-plan) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **spex-plan**: name: spex-plan description: Triggered when the user says "spex plan", "lets plan", or requests any code change. - **spex-plan**: SDD 流程中 spec 之後的第一站。讀取 work item description 的規格（spec-template 格式）做分類（需求/缺陷路線、Tier、缺陷規模）與輸入完整性檢核、建立工作分支，再產生技術實施計畫回寫 tracker 留言。Tier 1 直接開單張 Task 子卡跳過 task；缺陷 M/L 先分流 spex-fixbug。前置：規格已寫入 work item description（建議用 spex-write-spec）。後續：spex-task（Tier 2）/ spex-implement（Tier 1）。 ([bibirock/momo_shop](https://github.com/bibirock/momo_shop/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/spex-plan))
- **spex-prd**: Write or change the project's PRD — its overall goal and main capabilities — by interviewing the user. Use when the user wants to create, extend, or change the PRD, or to describe the product before writing specs. ([foreachpartners/main-spex](https://github.com/foreachpartners/main-spex/tree/HEAD/examples/shopping-notes/shopping-notes-spex/.agents/skills/spex-prd) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **spex-pull-request**: PR 開立唯一入口。前置 Gate 強制 selfcheck Verify PASS 與章戳稽核，組裝 PR 內容後開立 Pull Request，寫入稽核留言並完成任務鏈閉環。整個 SDD 流程中只有本 skill 允許呼叫 TRACKER.createPullRequest。開 PR 屬一般流程；合併一律由人類於平台 UI 執行。前置：spex-selfcheck（PASS）。後續：人類 reviewer。 ([bibirock/momo_shop](https://github.com/bibirock/momo_shop/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/spex-pull-request))
- **spex-reflect**: Analyzes a completed feature development cycle to identify missing context, confusing decisions, and propose actionable memory updates. Invoke manually after feature completion. ([ran729/context-rot-skill](https://github.com/ran729/context-rot-skill/tree/HEAD/.claude/.claude/skills/spex-reflect) · ★2)
- **spex-review**: Review the changes since a starting point (commit, branch, tag, or PR) along both axes at once — governing rules (does the code follow cross-cutting conventions and coding standards?) and specs (is the implementation of the spec's acceptance criteria correct?). Uses spex-review-rule and spex-review-spec skills. Use when the user wants to review a branch, spec, a PR, or work-in-progress changes. - **spex-review-rule**: Review the changes since a starting point (commit, branch, tag, or PR) against governing cross-cutting rules and coding standards. - **spex-review-spec**: Review the current implementation of a spec — is what is implemented of the spec's acceptance criteria correct? Use when the user wants to review implementation of a spec. ([foreachpartners/main-spex](https://github.com/foreachpartners/main-spex/tree/HEAD/examples/shopping-notes/shopping-notes-spex/.agents/skills/spex-review-spec) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **spex-roadmap**: 規格索引／產品 roadmap 檢視器。重建並顯示跨資料夾的規格總覽（specs/INDEX.md）：全部卡片、隸屬 Epic、目前階段、估點、最後更新， 讓「有哪些規格待執行、各自卡在哪一關」一眼可見。唯讀工具——只重寫衍生視圖 specs/INDEX.md，不改任何卡片內容。 前置：無（specs/ 有卡片即可）。後續：依索引挑卡片走 spex-plan / spex-implement。 ([bibirock/momo_shop](https://github.com/bibirock/momo_shop/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/spex-roadmap))
- **spex-rule**: Create a new rule, edit an existing one, or remove one by interviewing the user. ([foreachpartners/main-spex](https://github.com/foreachpartners/main-spex/tree/HEAD/examples/shopping-notes/shopping-notes-spex/.agents/skills/spex-rule) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **spex-schedule**: 批次排程執行器。指定多個卡片編號（或 tracker 查詢、local-file 的 specs/ 目錄等任務來源），自動執行時從 base 分支切出單一共用分支 `chore/schedule-&lt;YYYYMMDD-HHmm&gt;`（時間戳到分，同日可多批；續行依 tracker 留言記錄的分支沿用），整批所有卡片都在此分支上依序套用 SDD workflow（plan → task → implement → selfcheck），批次結束對帳通過後呼叫 spex-pull-request 開「一個」涵蓋全批的 PR 給使用者確認；每輪迭代重新從 tracker 盤點全部卡片狀態，以終態唯一性與結束前對帳保證不遺漏任何過程及任務；起跑序固定先以 /plan 唯讀盤點＋確認＋授權，核准後由執行驅動三選一推進（小批次手動即可；大批次或要無人值守跑到完用 /goal；卡片常等外部事件用 /loop），支援中斷續行與失敗隔離。前置：各卡片規格已寫入 description。後續：人類 reviewer。 ([bibirock/momo_shop](https://github.com/bibirock/momo_shop/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/spex-schedule))
- **spex-selfcheck**: 獨立驗收編排者。在 implement 完成後、開 PR 前，先跑確定性檢查（驗證指令束 + E2E），再派發全新上下文的獨立 AI 驗證者逐條 AC 對照 diff 與證據做二元判定；任一 Fail 即產出結構化重做清單退回 implement（最多 2 次重做，超限升級人工），全 PASS 才放行 spex-pull-request。前置：spex-implement。後續：spex-pull-request。 ([bibirock/momo_shop](https://github.com/bibirock/momo_shop/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/spex-selfcheck))
- **spex-setup**: Configure one or multiple new packages for usage with spex framework, define them in spex.yaml configuration, create package skill for each. - **spex-stamp**: 章戳鏈驗章器（確定性防偽閘）。在寫留言／開卡、階段交棒、開 PR 前稽核任何 challenge / verifier 的 PASS 宣稱：對本平面的事件流執行 challenge-audit.py，無章、章面不符、輪次超限、內容綁定不符、一章重用一律擋下。前置：challenge / verifier 已派發並留下事件紀錄。後續：回呼叫流程（task / implement / selfcheck / pull-request / schedule）。 ([bibirock/momo_shop](https://github.com/bibirock/momo_shop/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/spex-stamp))
- **spex-task**: 根據 tracker 相關留言區的 task 把實作工作拆解成可執行的 TDD 任務清單。用於垂直切片、定義 TDD 驗收標準，開卡後把任務依賴寫入 tracker 原生連結。前置：spex-plan（Tier 2）。後續：spex-implement。（Tier 1 輕量任務不走本 skill，由 spex-plan 直接開單張 Task 卡） ([bibirock/momo_shop](https://github.com/bibirock/momo_shop/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/spex-task))

- **spex-write-spec**: 需求分析師角色，透過問答與邊界探索把模糊想法寫成符合 spec-template 的結構化規格，供使用者貼入 tracker work item description；規格過大時依垂直切片最佳實踐拆成多張獨立可交付的 User Story、歸類於父 Epic（rollup），卡片數量不設上限。適用於開卡前撰寫規格、或補強既有 work item 的不完整規格。前置：無。後續：spex-plan（讀取 description 規格做分類）。 ([bibirock/momo_shop](https://github.com/bibirock/momo_shop/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/spex-write-spec))

- **spexd-authoring**: Author and curate the Spexd traceability chain — features, requirements, acceptance criteria, designs and tasks — via the Spexd MCP tools. - **spexd-implementing**: Implement work tracked in Spexd — pick up a task, read its traceability chain (design, acceptance criteria, requirement, feature) as the spec, check it is safe to start, build to it without changing it, and keep the task's lifecycle status in step with its branch and PR. Given a feature, requirement or design, build it task by task, one branch and one PR each. Use when asked to pick up, build, ship or find the next piece of Spexd work, or to turn a task into code. To write or change the spec itself, use spexd-authoring. ([forthfoundry/agent-skills](https://github.com/forthfoundry/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/spexd-implementing))
- **spexd-importing**: Bootstrap a whole Spexd project from an existing codebase and its supporting sources — reverse-engineer the feature set, then fan out one sub-agent per feature to derive requirements and acceptance criteria, then design across the requirements — all via the Spexd MCP tools (mcp__Spexd__*). Use when asked to import, onboard, backfill, or reverse-engineer an existing project/repo into Spexd, or to stand up the traceability chain for software that already exists. - **spexd-resolving**: Settle the findings of a completed Spexd review on a design or on a single acceptance criterion — put the findings to the reader as choices, correct the specification where the software is right, raise work where the software is wrong, publish as one version, and ask for the subject to be judged again, via the Spexd MCP tools (mcp__Spexd__*). - **spexl-apply**: Implement a proposed spec change -- write the code and the tests that satisfy every requirement and scenario. ([a3lem/spexl](https://github.com/a3lem/spexl/tree/HEAD/.shablon/templates/skills/spexl-apply) · 2 copies)
- **spexl-archive**: Archive a completed spexl change by merging its deltas into reference specs and moving the change to the archive directory. Use when the user asks to "archive", "merge deltas", "finalize", "complete a change", "wrap up", or references a change that has been applied and verified. Only archive when implementation is done and tests pass -- archiving a half-finished change leaves the reference specs inconsistent. ([a3lem/spexl](https://github.com/a3lem/spexl/tree/HEAD/.shablon/templates/skills/spexl-archive) · 2 copies)
- **spexl-explore**: Explore an idea, investigate a problem, or clarify requirements before committing to a formal spec change. Use when the user asks to "explore", "investigate", "think through", "research", "brainstorm", "understand the codebase", or wants a thinking partner before writing a proposal. - **spexl-foundations**: Foundational knowledge for spec-driven development with spexl. Load when the user asks "what is spec-driven development", "how do I use spexl", "what is a spec delta", "explain SDD", or any question about the workflow, concepts, rules, or artifacts. - **spexl-propose**: Propose a new change in a spexl project. Use when the user asks to "propose a change", "create a spec", "start a new feature", "define requirements", "add a capability", "write a proposal", or describes new functionality that should be specified before implementation. Produces a change directory with proposal.md, per-capability spec deltas, and optionally design.md and tasks.md. - **spexl-refine**: Update an existing artifact in a spexl change. Use when the user asks to "refine", "update the proposal", "modify the spec", "change requirements", "revise the design", "update tasks", or has feedback that changes scope, behavior, approach, or task breakdown of an active change.
